1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n\n      const color = starColorPalette[Math.floor(Math.random() * starColorPalette.length)];\n      starColors[i * 3] = color.r;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 1] = color.g;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 2] = color.b;\n    }\n\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new {\n        const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;\n        const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;\n        touchStartDist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n      }\n    };\n\n e.touches[1].clientY;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n        const factor = touchStartDist / dist;\n        spherical.radius = Math.max(15, Math.min(800, spherical.radius * (1 + (factor - window.addEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n    container.addEventListener('wheel', handleWheel, { passive: false });\n    container.addEventListener('touchstart', handleTouchStart, { camera.aspect = container.clientWidth / container.clientHeight;\n      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n      renderer.setSize(container.clientWidth, container.clientHeight);\n    };\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    // --- メインアニメーションループ ---\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n    let lastTime = performance.now();\n\n    const animate = () => {\n p.rotationAngle += p.data.rotationSpeed * speedMultiplier * delta * 60;\n          p.mesh.rotation.y = p.rotationAngle;\n\n          // 衛星の公転\n          if (p.moons) {\n            p.moons.forEach(m => {\n              m.angle += m.speed * speedMultiplier * delta * 60;\n              m.pivot.rotation.y = m.angle;\n            });\n          }\n        }\n      });\n\n      // 選択天体フォーカス追尾ロジック\n      if (selectedPlanetId) {\n        if (selectedPlanetId === 'sun') {\n          cameraTarget.set(0, 0, 0);\n          // 太陽選択時は適度なズームへ補正\n          if (spherical.radius > 70) {\n            spherical.radius = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(spherical.radius, 40, 0.05);\n          }\n        } else {\n          const targetObj = planetObjects.find(p => p.id === selectedPlanetId);\n          if カメラ注視点のスムーズな補間\n      cameraLookAt.lerp(cameraTarget, 0.08);\n\n      // カメラ位置の適用\n      updateCameraFromSpherical();\n\n      renderer.render(scene, camera);\n    };\n\n    animate();\n\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n      container.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n      container.removeEventListener('wheel', handleWheel);\n      container.removeEventListener('touchstart', handleTouchStart);\n      container.removeEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove);\n\n      if (container.contains(renderer.domElement)) {\n        container.removeChild(renderer.domElement);\n      }\n\n      // メモリ解放\n      starGeometry.dispose();\n      starMaterial.dispose();\n      sunGeometry.dispose();\n      sunMaterial.dispose();\n canvas.height = 128;\n  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n\n  if (ctx) {\n    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n\n    // 背景の半透明カプセル\n    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(3, 7, 18, 0.75)';\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);\n    ctx.lineTo(x + w - r, y);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r);\n    ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h - r);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - r, y + h);\n    ctx.lineTo(x + r, y + h);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - r);\n    ctx.lineTo(x, y + r);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y);\n    ctx.closePath();\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.stroke();\n\n    // メイン天体名\n    ctx.font = 'bold 28px -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, sans-serif';\n    ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n    ctx.fillText(text, canvas.width / 2, 48);\n\n    // 英語名\n    ctx.font = '500 18px ui-monospace, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, monospace';\n    ctx.fillStyle = scene.add(sunLight);\n\n    // 補助微光ライト（太陽の真裏が暗黒になりすぎないよう調整）\n    const fillLight = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0x1e293b, 0x020617, 0.4);\n    scene.add(fillLight);\n\n    // --- 星空背景パーティクル ---\n < starCount; i++) {\n      const radius = 600 + Math.random() * 800;\n      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const phi = Math.acos(Math.random() * 2 - 1);\n\n      starPositions[i * 3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n\n      const color = starColorPalette[Math.floor(Math.random() * starColorPalette.length)];\n      starColors[i * 3] = color.r;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 1] = color.g;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 2] = color.b;\n    }\n\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starColors, 3));\n\n    const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n      size: 1.8,\n      vertexColors: true,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.85\n    });\n    const starField = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, planetHitMesh.position.set(data.distance, 0, 0);\n      planetHitMesh.userData = { id: data.id };\n      pivot.add(planetHitMesh);\n      interactiveMeshes.push(planetHitMesh);\n\n      // 天王星などの自転軸傾斜角\n      if (data.id === 'uranus') {\n        planetMesh.rotation.z = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(97.7);\n      } else if (data.id === 'earth') {\n        planetMesh.rotation.z = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(23.4);\n      } else if (data.id === 'mars') {\n        planetMesh.rotation.z = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(25.2);\n      } else if (data.id === 'saturn') {\n        planetMesh.rotation.z = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(26.7);\n      }\n\n      let ringMesh: THREE.Mesh | undefined;\n      // 土星または天王星のリング\n      if (data.ring) {\n        const ringGeo = new THREE.Mesh(moonGeo, moonMat);\n          moonMesh.position.set(m.distance, 0, 0);\n          moonPivot.add(moonMesh);\n\n          moonsList?.push({\n            mesh: moonMesh,\n            pivot: labelSprite,\n        orbitAngle: initialAngle,\n        rotationAngle: 0,\n        ringMesh,\n        moons: moonsList\n      });\n    });\n\n    // --- カメラ操作・インタラクション状態 ---\n    const cameraTarget = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n    const cameraLookAt = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n\n    let isPointerDown = false;\n    let hasPointerMoved = false;\n    let pointerStartX = 0;\n    let pointerStartY = 0;\n    const spherical = new THREE.Spherical(200, Math.PI / 3.2, 0);\n\n    let touchStartDist = 0;\n\n    // 選択天体ワールド座標追従用\n    const currentPlanetWorldPos = new THREE.Vector3();\n\n    const updateCameraFromSpherical = () => {\n      const offset = new THREE.Vector3().setFromSpherical(spherical);\n      camera.position.copy(cameraLookAt).add(offset);\n      camera.lookAt(cameraLookAt);\n    };\n    updateCameraFromSpherical();\n\n    // マウス・タッチ制御イベント\n    const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      isPointerDown = true;\n      hasPointerMoved = false;\n      pointerStartX = e.clientX;\n      pointerStartY = e.clientY;\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (!isPointerDown) return;\n      const deltaX = e.clientX - pointerStartX;\n      const deltaY = e.clientY - pointerStartY;\n\n      if (Math.abs(deltaX) > 4 || Math.abs(deltaY) > 4) {\n        hasPointerMoved = true;\n      }\n\n      spherical.theta -= deltaX * 0.005;\n      spherical.phi = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(Math.PI / 2.05, spherical.phi - deltaY * 0.005));\n\n      pointerStartX = e.clientX;\n      pointerStartY = e.clientY;\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerUp = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (isPointerDown && !hasPointerMoved) {\n        // クリック/タップ判定 -> Raycaster\n        const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n        const x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;\n        const y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;\n\n        const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();\n        raycaster.setFromCamera(new THREE.Vector2(x, y), camera);\n\n        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(interactiveMeshes, false);\n        if (intersects.length > 0) {\n          const hit = intersects[0].object;\n          const hitId = hit.userData.id as string | undefined;\n          if (hitId) {\n            onSelectPlanetRef.current(hitId);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n      isPointerDown = false;\n    };\n\n    const handleWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      spherical.radius = Math.max(15, Math.min(800, spherical.radius + e.deltaY * 0.25));\n    };\n\n    // タッチピンチズーム対応\n    const handleTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => {\n      if (e.touches.length === 2) {\n        const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;\n        const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;\n        touchStartDist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => {\n      if (e.touches.length === 2) {\n        const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;\n        const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n        const factor = touchStartDist / dist;\n        spherical.radius = Math.max(15, Math.min(800, spherical.radius * (1 + (factor - 1) * 0.1)));\n        touchStartDist = dist;\n      }\n    };\n\n    container.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n    window.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n    container.addEventListener('wheel', handleWheel, { passive: false });\n    container.addEventListener('touchstart', handleTouchStart, { passive: true });\n    container.addEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove, { passive: true });\n\n    // ウィンドウリサイズ対応\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!container) return;\n      camera.aspect = container.clientWidth / container.clientHeight;\n      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n      renderer.setSize(container.clientWidth, container.clientHeight);\n    };\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    // --- メインアニメーションループ ---\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n    let lastTime = performance.now();\n    const tempVec = new THREE.Vector3();\n\n    const animate = () => {\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n      const currentTime = performance.now();\n      const delta = Math.min((currentTime - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);\n      lastTime = currentTime;\n\n      const { isPlaying, speedMultiplier, showOrbits, showLabels, selectedPlanetId } = settingsRef.current;\n\n      // 軌道線およびラベル表示状態の反映\n      orbitLinesGroup.visible = showOrbits;\n      labelsGroup.visible = showLabels;\n\n      // 太陽自転\n      if (isPlaying) {\n        sunMesh.rotation.y += SUN_DATA.rotationSpeed * speedMultiplier;\n        sunGlow.rotation.y += SUN_DATA.rotationSpeed * 0.5 * speedMultiplier;\n      }\n\n      // 惑星公転・自転\n      planetObjects.forEach(p => {\n        if (isPlaying) {\n          p.orbitAngle += p.data.speed * speedMultiplier * delta * 60;\n          p.pivot.rotation.y = p.orbitAngle;\n\n          p.rotationAngle += p.data.rotationSpeed * speedMultiplier * delta * 60;\n          p.mesh.rotation.y = p.rotationAngle;\n\n          // 衛星の公転\n          if (p.moons) {\n            p.moons.forEach(m => {\n              m.angle += m.speed * speedMultiplier * delta * 60;\n              m.pivot.rotation.y = m.angle;\n            });\n          }\n        }\n\n        // ラベル位置のリアルタイム更新（自転で回転しないよう親ではなくlabelsGroup内で追随）\n        p.mesh.getWorldPosition(tempVec);\n        p.labelSprite.position.set(tempVec.x, tempVec.y + p.data.radius + 2.2, tempVec.z);\n      });\n\n      // 選択天体フォーカス追尾ロジック\n      if (selectedPlanetId) {\n        if (selectedPlanetId === 'sun') {\n          cameraTarget.set(0, 0, 0);\n          if (spherical.radius > 70) {\n            spherical.radius = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(spherical.radius, 40, 0.05);\n          }\n        } else {\n          const targetObj = planetObjects.find(p => p.id === selectedPlanetId);\n          if (targetObj) {\n            targetObj.mesh.getWorldPosition(currentPlanetWorldPos);\n            cameraTarget.copy(currentPlanetWorldPos);\n\n            // 惑星の大きさに合わせた適切な観測距離へスムーズにズーム\n            const desiredRadius = Math.max(targetObj.data.radius * 5.5, 16);\n            if (Math.abs(spherical.radius - desiredRadius) > 0.5) {\n              spherical.radius = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(spherical.radius, desiredRadius, 0.06);\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      } else {\n        // 未選択時は太陽系全体中央を注視\n        cameraTarget.set(0, 0, 0);\n      }\n\n      // カメラ注視点のスムーズな補間\n      cameraLookAt.lerp(cameraTarget, 0.08);\n\n      // カメラ位置の適用\n      updateCameraFromSpherical();\n\n      renderer.render(scene, camera);\n    };\n\n    animate();\n\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n      container.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n      container.removeEventListener('wheel', handleWheel);\n      container.removeEventListener('touchstart', handleTouchStart);\n      container.removeEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove);\n\n      if (container.contains(renderer.domElement)) {\n        container.removeChild(renderer.domElement);\n      }\n\n      // 包括的リソース解放（メモリリーク防止）\n      scene.traverse((obj) => {\n        const meshObj = obj as THREE.Mesh;\n        if (meshObj.geometry) {\n          meshObj.geometry.dispose();\n        }\n        if (meshObj.material) {\n          if (Array.isArray(meshObj.material)) {\n            meshObj.material.forEach((mat) => {\n              if ('map' in mat && mat.map) {\n                mat.map.dispose();\n              }\n              mat.dispose();\n            });\n          } else {\n            const singleMat = meshObj.material as THREE.Material & { map?: THREE.Texture };\n            if (singleMat.map) {\n              singleMat.map.dispose();\n            }\n            singleMat.dispose();\n          }\n        }\n      });\n\n      hitMat.dispose();\n      renderer.dispose();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return <div ref={mountRef} className=\"canvas-container\" />;\n};\n\nexport default SolarCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

canvas.height = 128;\n  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n\n  if (ctx) {\n    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n\n    // 背景の半透明カプセル\n    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(3, 7, 18, 0.75)';\n    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4)';\n    ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n    const x = 16;\n    const y = 16;\n    const w = canvas.width - 32;\n    const h = canvas.height - 32;\n    const r = 24;\n\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);\n    ctx.lineTo(x + w - r, y);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r);\n    ctx.lineTo(x + w, y + h - r);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - r, y + h);\n    ctx.lineTo(x + r, y + h);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - r);\n    ctx.lineTo(x, y + r);\n    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y);\n    ctx.closePath();\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.stroke();\n\n    // メイン天体名\n    ctx.font = 'bold 28px -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, sans-serif';\n    ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n    ctx.fillText(text, canvas.width / 2, 48);\n\n    // 英語名\n    ctx.font = '500 18px ui-monospace, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, monospace';\n    ctx.fillStyle = scene.add(sunLight);\n\n    // 補助微光ライト（太陽の真裏が暗黒になりすぎないよう調整）\n    const fillLight = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0x1e293b, 0x020617, 0.4);\n    scene.add(fillLight);\n\n    // --- 星空背景パーティクル ---\n < starCount; i++) {\n      const radius = 600 + Math.random() * 800;\n      const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const phi = Math.acos(Math.random() * 2 - 1);\n\n      starPositions[i * 3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      starPositions[i * 3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n\n      const color = starColorPalette[Math.floor(Math.random() * starColorPalette.length)];\n      starColors[i * 3] = color.r;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 1] = color.g;\n      starColors[i * 3 + 2] = color.b;\n    }\n\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starColors, 3));\n\n    const planetHitMesh.position.set(data.distance, 0, 0);\n      planetHitMesh.userData = { id: data.id };\n      pivot.add(planetHitMesh);\n      interactiveMeshes.push(planetHitMesh);\n\n      // THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(23.4);\n      } else if (data.id === 'mars') {\n        planetMesh.rotation.z = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(25.2);\n      } else if (data.id === 'saturn') {\n        planetMesh.rotation.z = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(26.7);\n      }\n\n      let ringMesh: THREE.Mesh | undefined;\n      // 土星または天王星のリング\n      if (data.ring) {\n        const ringGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(data.ring.innerRadius, data.ring.outerRadius, 64);\n        const ringMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(data.ring.color),\n          side: THREE.DoubleSide,\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.8,\n          roughness: 0.5\n        });\n        ringMesh = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeo, ringMat);\n        ringMesh.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;\n        planetMesh.add(ringMesh);\n      }\n\n      // 衛星（地球の月など）\n      let moonsList: PlanetObjectRef['moons'] = undefined;\n      if (data.moons && data.moons.length > 0) {\n        moonsList = [];\n        data.moons.forEach(m => {\n          const moonPivot = new THREE.Group();\n          planetMesh.add(moonPivot);\n\n          const moonGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(m.radius, 16, 16);\n          const moonMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n            color: new THREE.Color(m.color),\n            roughness: 0.9\n          });\n          const moonMesh = new THREE.Mesh(moonGeo, moonMat);\n          moonMesh.position.set(m.distance, 0, 0);\n          moonPivot.add(moonMesh);\n\n          moonsList?.push({\n            mesh: moonMesh,\n            pivot: moonPivot,\n            angle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,\n            speed: m.speed\n          });\n        });\n      }\n\n      // 惑星名ラベルスプライト\n      const labelSprite = createTextSprite(data.name, data.englishName, data.color);\n      labelsGroup.add(labelSprite);\n\n      // 初期公転角度をばらけさせる\n      const initialAngle = (index * (Math.PI * 2)) / PLANETS_DATA.length + 0.3;\n      pivot.rotation.y = initialAngle;\n\n      planetObjects.push({\n        id: data.id,\n        data,\n        pivot,\n        mesh: planetMesh,\n        hitMesh: planetHitMesh,\n        labelSprite,\n        orbitAngle: initialAngle,\n        rotationAngle: 0,\n        ringMesh,\n        moons: moonsList\n      });\n    });\n\n    // --- カメラ操作・インタラクション状態 ---\n    const cameraTarget = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n    const cameraLookAt = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);\n\n    let isPointerDown = false;\n    let hasPointerMoved = false;\n    let pointerStartX = 0;\n    let pointerStartY = 0;\n    const spherical = new THREE.Spherical(200, Math.PI / 3.2, 0);\n\n    let touchStartDist = 0;\n\n    // 選択天体ワールド座標追従用\n    const currentPlanetWorldPos = new THREE.Vector3();\n\n    const updateCameraFromSpherical = () => {\n      const offset = new THREE.Vector3().setFromSpherical(spherical);\n      camera.position.copy(cameraLookAt).add(offset);\n      camera.lookAt(cameraLookAt);\n    };\n    updateCameraFromSpherical();\n\n    // マウス・タッチ制御イベント\n    const handlePointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      isPointerDown = true;\n      hasPointerMoved = false;\n      pointerStartX = e.clientX;\n      pointerStartY = e.clientY;\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (!isPointerDown) return;\n      const deltaX = e.clientX - pointerStartX;\n      const deltaY = e.clientY - pointerStartY;\n\n      if (Math.abs(deltaX) > 4 || Math.abs(deltaY) > 4) {\n        hasPointerMoved = true;\n      }\n\n      spherical.theta -= deltaX * 0.005;\n      spherical.phi = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(Math.PI / 2.05, spherical.phi - deltaY * 0.005));\n\n      pointerStartX = e.clientX;\n      pointerStartY = e.clientY;\n    };\n\n    const handlePointerUp = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (isPointerDown && !hasPointerMoved) {\n        // クリック/タップ判定 -> Raycaster\n        const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n        const x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;\n        const y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;\n\n        const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();\n        raycaster.setFromCamera(new THREE.Vector2(x, y), camera);\n\n        const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(interactiveMeshes, false);\n        if (intersects.length > 0) {\n          const hit = intersects[0].object;\n          const hitId = hit.userData.id as string | undefined;\n          if (hitId) {\n            onSelectPlanetRef.current(hitId);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n      isPointerDown = false;\n    };\n\n    const handleWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      spherical.radius = Math.max(15, Math.min(800, spherical.radius + e.deltaY * 0.25));\n    };\n\n    // タッチピンチズーム対応\n    const handleTouchStart = (e: TouchEvent) => {\n      if (e.touches.length === 2) {\n        const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;\n        const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;\n        touchStartDist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleTouchMove = (e: TouchEvent) => {\n      if (e.touches.length === 2) {\n        const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;\n        const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n        const factor = touchStartDist / dist;\n        spherical.radius = Math.max(15, Math.min(800, spherical.radius * (1 + (factor - 1) * 0.1)));\n        touchStartDist = dist;\n      }\n    };\n\n    container.addEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n    window.addEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n    container.addEventListener('wheel', handleWheel, { passive: false });\n    container.addEventListener('touchstart', handleTouchStart, { passive: true });\n    container.addEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove, { passive: true });\n\n    // ウィンドウリサイズ対応\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!container) return;\n      camera.aspect = container.clientWidth / container.clientHeight;\n      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n      renderer.setSize(container.clientWidth, container.clientHeight);\n    };\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    // --- メインアニメーションループ ---\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n    let lastTime = performance.now();\n    const tempVec = new THREE.Vector3();\n\n    const animate = () => {\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n      const currentTime = performance.now();\n      const delta = Math.min((currentTime - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);\n      lastTime = currentTime;\n\n      const { isPlaying, speedMultiplier, showOrbits, showLabels, selectedPlanetId } = settingsRef.current;\n\n      // 軌道線およびラベル表示状態の反映\n      orbitLinesGroup.visible = showOrbits;\n      labelsGroup.visible = showLabels;\n\n      // 太陽自転\n      if (isPlaying) {\n        sunMesh.rotation.y += SUN_DATA.rotationSpeed * speedMultiplier;\n        sunGlow.rotation.y += SUN_DATA.rotationSpeed * 0.5 * speedMultiplier;\n      }\n\n      // 惑星公転・自転\n      planetObjects.forEach(p => {\n        if (isPlaying) {\n          p.orbitAngle += p.data.speed * speedMultiplier * delta * 60;\n          p.pivot.rotation.y = p.orbitAngle;\n\n          p.rotationAngle += p.data.rotationSpeed * speedMultiplier * delta * 60;\n          p.mesh.rotation.y = p.rotationAngle;\n\n          // 衛星の公転\n          if (p.moons) {\n            p.moons.forEach(m => {\n              m.angle += m.speed * speedMultiplier * delta * 60;\n              m.pivot.rotation.y = m.angle;\n            });\n          }\n        }\n\n        // ラベル位置のリアルタイム更新（自転で回転しないよう親ではなくlabelsGroup内で追随）\n        p.mesh.getWorldPosition(tempVec);\n        p.labelSprite.position.set(tempVec.x, tempVec.y + p.data.radius + 2.2, tempVec.z);\n      });\n\n      // 選択天体フォーカス追尾ロジック\n      if (selectedPlanetId) {\n        if (selectedPlanetId === 'sun') {\n          cameraTarget.set(0, 0, 0);\n          const desiredRadius = SUN_DATA.focusDistance ?? 40;\n          if (Math.abs(spherical.radius - desiredRadius) > 0.5) {\n            spherical.radius = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(spherical.radius, desiredRadius, 0.05);\n          }\n        } else {\n          const targetObj = planetObjects.find(p => p.id === selectedPlanetId);\n          if (targetObj) {\n            targetObj.mesh.getWorldPosition(currentPlanetWorldPos);\n            cameraTarget.copy(currentPlanetWorldPos);\n\n            // 惑星定義の最適フォーカス距離を優先参照し、未定義なら大きさから算出\n            const desiredRadius = targetObj.data.focusDistance ?? Math.max(targetObj.data.radius * 5.5, 16);\n            if (Math.abs(spherical.radius - desiredRadius) > 0.5) {\n              spherical.radius = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(spherical.radius, desiredRadius, 0.06);\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      } else {\n        // 未選択時は太陽系全体中央を注視\n        cameraTarget.set(0, 0, 0);\n      }\n\n      // カメラ注視点のスムーズな補間\n      cameraLookAt.lerp(cameraTarget, 0.08);\n\n      // カメラ位置の適用\n      updateCameraFromSpherical();\n\n      renderer.render(scene, camera);\n    };\n\n    animate();\n\n    return () => {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n      container.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handlePointerDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', handlePointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', handlePointerUp);\n      container.removeEventListener('wheel', handleWheel);\n      container.removeEventListener('touchstart', handleTouchStart);\n      container.removeEventListener('touchmove', handleTouchMove);\n\n      if (container.contains(renderer.domElement)) {\n        container.removeChild(renderer.domElement);\n      }\n\n      // 包括的リソース解放（メモリリーク防止）\n      scene.traverse((obj) => {\n        const meshObj = obj as THREE.Mesh;\n        if (meshObj.geometry) {\n          meshObj.geometry.dispose();\n        }\n        if (meshObj.material) {\n          if (Array.isArray(meshObj.material)) {\n            meshObj.material.forEach((mat) => {\n              if ('map' in mat && mat.map) {\n                mat.map.dispose();\n              }\n              mat.dispose();\n            });\n          } else {\n            const singleMat = meshObj.material as THREE.Material & { map?: THREE.Texture };\n            if (singleMat.map) {\n              singleMat.map.dispose();\n            }\n            singleMat.dispose();\n          }\n        }\n      });\n\n      hitMat.dispose();\n      renderer.dispose();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return <div ref={mountRef} className=\"canvas-container\" />;\n};\n\nexport default SolarCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

setSettings((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      selectedPlanetId: null\n    }));\n    setResetTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);\n  }, []);\n\n  // グローバルキーボードショートカット {\n        const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX;\n        const dy = e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY;\n        touchStartDist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n      }\n    };\n\n
